* Start of Frame
	Ini : Set current file to "savegame"
	delay count : Set Counter to 100

* User clicks with left button on Group.Bombs
	Group.Bombs : Change animation sequence to Stand up

* Always

* User clicks with left button on back
	storyboard controls : Jump to frame "Cloud saves" (64)

* User clicks with left button on previous page
	storyboard controls : Jump to frame "Transfer to cloud" (65)

* Android object : On back button pressed
	storyboard controls : Jump to frame "Transfer to cloud" (65)
	back : Change animation sequence to Stand up

* AndroidPlus: On App return from pause?
	Special : Set game resumed to 1

* delay count > 0
	delay count : Subtract 1 from Counter
